<h2 class="kt-adv-heading1244_c98b79-f0 wp-block-kadence-advancedheading" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading1244_c98b79-f0">TITANS GAMES</h2>



<div class="wp-block-kadence-accordion alignnone"><div class="kt-accordion-wrap kt-accordion-id1244_8f5c84-c3 kt-accordion-has-16-panes kt-active-pane-0 kt-accordion-block kt-pane-header-alignment-left kt-accodion-icon-style-arrow kt-accodion-icon-side-right" style="max-width:none"><div class="kt-accordion-inner-wrap" data-allow-multiple-open="false" data-start-open="none">
<div class="wp-block-kadence-pane kt-accordion-pane kt-accordion-pane-1 kt-pane1244_3a59b1-1c"><div class="kt-accordion-header-wrap"><button class="kt-blocks-accordion-header kt-acccordion-button-label-show" type="button"><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-title-wrap"><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-title">ParkING</span></span><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-icon-trigger"></span></button></div><div class="kt-accordion-panel kt-accordion-panel-hidden" tabindex="0"><div class="kt-accordion-panel-inner">
<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column1244_8b67eb-81"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<p class="wp-block-paragraph">On-site parking for Titans games is sold out. There are no day-of sales available on campus.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Explore nearby parking options:</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li>Fans are now able to purchase single game parking here <a href="https://park.parkingmgt.com/venues/nissan-stadium-nashville-tn" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">here<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a>.</li>



<li>Use the interactive campus map to view and book available PMC parking lots until they open for each event. <a href="https://qr.us.app.pam.co/s/5d493179" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Start here<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a>, find a lot, and click “Book Now.”</li>
</ul>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Guests with Nissan Stadium parking passes can access turn-by-turn directions through <a href="https://qr.us.app.pam.co/s/5d493179" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">the interactive campus map here<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a>.</p>



<p class="kt-adv-heading1244_44a9c8-59 wp-block-kadence-advancedheading" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading1244_44a9c8-59"> </p>



<p class="kt-adv-heading1244_e31ff3-74 wp-block-kadence-advancedheading" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading1244_e31ff3-74"><em>Note: Each Titans issued parking pass is valid for a single space in the designated lot.</em></p>
</div></div>
</div></div></div>



<div class="wp-block-kadence-pane kt-accordion-pane kt-accordion-pane-9 kt-pane1244_7a5450-7b"><div class="kt-accordion-header-wrap"><button class="kt-blocks-accordion-header kt-acccordion-button-label-show" type="button"><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-title-wrap"><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-title">WALK</span></span><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-icon-trigger"></span></button></div><div class="kt-accordion-panel kt-accordion-panel-hidden" tabindex="0"><div class="kt-accordion-panel-inner">
<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column1244_934762-43"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Pedestrian access is available via multiple bridges connecting downtown and surrounding areas:</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><a href="https://qr.us.app.pam.co/s/1169572c" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Woodland Street Bridge<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a> <em>(recommended for fastest access)</em></li>



<li><a href="https://qr.us.app.pam.co/s/e241df24" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Korean Veterans Blvd Bridge<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a></li>



<li><a href="https://qr.us.app.pam.co/s/e6dcbb49" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">John Seigenthaler Pedestrian Bridge<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a></li>
</ul>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Walking is also available from East Nashville (Woodland St. &amp; Shelby Ave) and N. 1st Street.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph"><em>Note: Pedestrian routes may be impacted by construction. Follow posted signage.</em></p>
</div></div>
</div></div></div>



<div class="wp-block-kadence-pane kt-accordion-pane kt-accordion-pane-6 kt-pane1244_9efd61-75"><div class="kt-accordion-header-wrap"><button class="kt-blocks-accordion-header kt-acccordion-button-label-show" type="button"><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-title-wrap"><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-title">RIDESHARE</span></span><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-icon-trigger"></span></button></div><div class="kt-accordion-panel kt-accordion-panel-hidden" tabindex="0"><div class="kt-accordion-panel-inner">
<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column1244_b329c2-1e"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Several designated rideshare pick-up and drop-off locations are available within walking distance of the stadium. The two closest locations are <a href="https://short-url.us.app.pam.co/s/3cl4euvl" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Crutcher St on the South end<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a> and <a href="https://short-url.us.app.pam.co/s/hz64vfj0" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Gay St on the North end<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a> across Woodland St Bridge. More than a dozen additional designated zones are located on the downtown side of the river. Following each event, please follow posted signage or staff direction to access these locations safely.</p>



<p class="kt-adv-heading1244_98d5ea-c2 wp-block-kadence-advancedheading" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading1244_98d5ea-c2"><a href="https://nam12.safelinks.protection.outlook.com/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fshort-url.us.app.pam.co%2Fs%2Fcb9gc631&amp;data=05%7C02%7C%7Cf3bc18d0a821493a364408dec8339a99%7C7cd6ae7eca8c40838198ba5c0376357d%7C0%7C0%7C639168321146559554%7CUnknown%7CTWFpbGZsb3d8eyJFbXB0eU1hcGkiOnRydWUsIlYiOiIwLjAuMDAwMCIsIlAiOiJXaW4zMiIsIkFOIjoiTWFpbCIsIldUIjoyfQ%3D%3D%7C0%7C%7C%7C&amp;sdata=cAFO99PdrcP%2F%2FvOY4WtW9roYZd6hoTzPrey1NN6HcS0%3D&amp;reserved=0" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">See all designated Rideshare Zones here.<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a></p>

<div class="wp-block-kadence-pane kt-accordion-pane kt-accordion-pane-12 kt-pane1244_bf60ed-69"><div class="kt-accordion-header-wrap"><button class="kt-blocks-accordion-header kt-acccordion-button-label-show" type="button"><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-title-wrap"><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-title">ELECTRIC SCOOTERS</span></span><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-icon-trigger"></span></button></div><div class="kt-accordion-panel kt-accordion-panel-hidden" tabindex="0"><div class="kt-accordion-panel-inner">

<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Signage and scooter company representatives will direct fans where to leave and how to collect their scooter upon departure. Scooters are not permitted inside stadium perimeter areas.</p>

<div class="wp-block-kadence-pane kt-accordion-pane kt-accordion-pane-8 kt-pane1244_a227be-84"><div class="kt-accordion-header-wrap"><button class="kt-blocks-accordion-header kt-acccordion-button-label-show" type="button"><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-title-wrap"><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-title">BIKE</span></span><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-icon-trigger"></span></button></div><div class="kt-accordion-panel kt-accordion-panel-hidden" tabindex="0"><div class="kt-accordion-panel-inner">

<p class="kt-adv-heading1244_f8afe6-6a wp-block-kadence-advancedheading" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading1244_f8afe6-6a">Bike racks are available&nbsp;<a href="https://qr.us.app.pam.co/s/e59c8f91" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">across the street from Gate 6<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a>, <a href="https://qr.us.app.pam.co/s/cf3b1943" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">near Lot T<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a>, and <a href="https://short-url.us.app.pam.co/s/04w6ye61" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">on Woodland St near the pedestrian stairs<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a>. <br>&nbsp;<br><strong>Bike Valet by Walk Bike Nashville</strong><br>The Titans have partnered with&nbsp;<a href="https://www.walkbikenashville.org/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Walk Bike Nashville<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a>&nbsp;to provide free Bike Valet for all fans who ride their bikes to Nissan Stadium for Titans home games.</p>



<p class="kt-adv-heading1244_c158ea-53 wp-block-kadence-advancedheading" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading1244_c158ea-53"><br>A blue Walk Bike Nashville tent will be set up at&nbsp;S. 1st St across from the foot of the pedestrian bridge&nbsp;.&nbsp;Bikes can be checked in up to two hours prior to kickoff and checked out up to one hour after the end of each game. Fans using bike valet do not need to bring a bike lock.</p>

<div class="wp-block-kadence-pane kt-accordion-pane kt-accordion-pane-13 kt-pane1244_037462-56"><div class="kt-accordion-header-wrap"><button class="kt-blocks-accordion-header kt-acccordion-button-label-show" type="button"><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-title-wrap"><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-title">SHUTTLEs</span></span><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-icon-trigger"></span></button></div><div class="kt-accordion-panel kt-accordion-panel-hidden" tabindex="0"><div class="kt-accordion-panel-inner">

<p class="kt-adv-heading1244_fd8f0a-a5 wp-block-kadence-advancedheading" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading1244_fd8f0a-a5"><strong>GRAY LINE TENNESSEE GAMEDAY SHUTTLE</strong></p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Enjoy a convenient and stress-free ride to Nissan Stadium with the <a href="https://graylinetn.com/gray-line-tennessee-game-day-shuttle/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Gray Line Tennessee Gameday Shuttle<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a>.</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">

<li>Return service begins at halftime through 1 hour post-game</li>



<li>Cost: $20 roundtrip per person (receipt serves as ticket)
<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li>Children 6 and under ride free</li>
</ul>
</li>



<li>Cash is not accepted; QR codes will be available on-site for purchase</li>
</ul>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph"><em>Please note: Shuttle ticket purchase does not guarantee parking availability.</em></p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph"><a href="https://graylinetn.com/gray-line-tennessee-game-day-shuttle/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Purchase Gray Line Tennessee Gameday Shuttle Tickets here.<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a></p>



<p class="kt-adv-heading1244_b68a80-7f wp-block-kadence-advancedheading" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading1244_b68a80-7f"><br><strong>NEW! PARK &amp; RIDE SHUTTLE WITH GRAY LINE &amp; PMC</strong></p>



<p class="kt-adv-heading1244_365334-17_0 wp-block-kadence-advancedheading" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading1244_365334-17_0">Gray Line Tennessee and PMC offer convenient gameday park &amp; ride shuttle options from downtown Nashville and Franklin/Cool Springs to and from Nissan Stadium.</p>



<p class="kt-adv-heading1244_846749-c4 wp-block-kadence-advancedheading" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading1244_846749-c4"></p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li>Locations: 1221 Broadway &amp; 1800 Galleria Blvd.</li>



<li>Cost: $50 per vehicle, plus taxes (1221 Broadway) &amp; $70 per vehicle, plus taxes (Franklin/Cool Springs)</li>



<li>Departure Times: 10:00 AM and 11:00 AM</li>



<li>Stadium Drop-Off &amp; Return Boarding: <a href="https://short-url.us.app.pam.co/s/zflkwbdl">Woodland St. and S. 1st St.</a></li>



<li>Tickets: Your parking lot receipt serves as your ticket. Please have it ready to show when parking and boarding. Purchasing in advance is encouraged.</li>
</ul>



<p class="kt-adv-heading1244_365334-17_6 wp-block-kadence-advancedheading" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading1244_365334-17_6"><a href="https://graylinetn.com/gray-line-tennessee-game-day-shuttle/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Purchase Gameday Park &amp; Ride Tickets here.<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a></p>

<div class="wp-block-kadence-pane kt-accordion-pane kt-accordion-pane-7 kt-pane1244_5664ae-7e"><div class="kt-accordion-header-wrap"><button class="kt-blocks-accordion-header kt-acccordion-button-label-show" type="button"><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-title-wrap"><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-title">WEGO PUBLIC TRANSIT</span></span><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-icon-trigger"></span></button></div><div class="kt-accordion-panel kt-accordion-panel-hidden" tabindex="0"><div class="kt-accordion-panel-inner">
<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column1244_f44573-f1"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Public transport through WeGo provides a convenient service to and from Titans games at Nissan Stadium. There are three ways you can roll with WeGo, including FREE local bus service, an Express Train with stops east of downtown from Donelson to Lebanon, and Park &amp; Ride Express Bus from four surrounding areas – Bellevue and Hickory Hollow (Free), Cool Springs and Gallatin/Hendersonville ($2 each way). In total, fans can choose from nine local and express routes connecting directly to Nissan Stadium. Bus drop-off and pick-up is on Woodland Street and S. 1st St., by the bridge, with WeGo staff on-site to help. </p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Visit <a href="https://www.wegotransit.com/community/nissan-stadium/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">WeGoTransit.com/Community/Nissan-Stadium<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a> for all the details!</p>
</div></div>
</div></div></div>



<div class="wp-block-kadence-pane kt-accordion-pane kt-accordion-pane-14 kt-pane1244_b39fbf-f4"><div class="kt-accordion-header-wrap"><button class="kt-blocks-accordion-header kt-acccordion-button-label-show" type="button"><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-title-wrap"><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-title">Water Transportation</span></span><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-icon-trigger"></span></button></div><div class="kt-accordion-panel kt-accordion-panel-hidden" tabindex="0"><div class="kt-accordion-panel-inner">
<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column1244_a5c585-5f"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Several Cumberland River operators offer boat service to the stadium on Titans gamedays, docking within walking distance of the venue. Schedules and pricing vary by operator.</p>



<p class="kt-adv-heading1244_8b52ef-3c wp-block-kadence-advancedheading" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading1244_8b52ef-3c"><br><em>Search “Cumberland River water transportation” for current providers, availability, schedules, and ticketing. Details may vary by event and operator. Not affiliated with or operated by Nissan Stadium or the Tennessee Titans.</em></p>
</div></div>
</div></div></div>



<div class="wp-block-kadence-pane kt-accordion-pane kt-accordion-pane-10 kt-pane1244_49e44a-a8"><div class="kt-accordion-header-wrap"><button class="kt-blocks-accordion-header kt-acccordion-button-label-show" type="button"><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-title-wrap"><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-title">ACCESSIBLE OPTIONS</span></span><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-icon-trigger"></span></button></div><div class="kt-accordion-panel kt-accordion-panel-hidden" tabindex="0"><div class="kt-accordion-panel-inner">
<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column1244_712f8a-ac"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<p class="wp-block-paragraph" id="accessible-titans-parking"><strong>Accessible Parking</strong></p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Accessible parking is available in stadium lots with a valid parking pass and is offered on a first-come, first-served basis. Parking passes must be&nbsp;<a href="https://seatgeek.com/venues/nissan-stadium-1-titans-way/parking" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">purchased in advance<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a>.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph"><strong>Accessible Drop-Off &amp; Pick-Up in Lot T</strong></p>



<p class="kt-adv-heading1244_6601c7-8d wp-block-kadence-advancedheading" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading1244_6601c7-8d"> The ADA drop-off and pick-up is <a href="https://qr.us.app.pam.co/s/e101ebfc" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">located in Lot T<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a>. Directions:</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li>Travel southbound on S. 5th St from Shelby Ave</li>



<li>Turn right onto Davidson St and proceed to Lot T</li>
</ul>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Please have your stadium-issued ADA Lane Pass ready for law enforcement at roadblocks. To receive a stadium-issued ADA Lane pass, please&nbsp;<a href="mailto:stadiumexperienceinfo@titans.nfl.com">send us a message</a>&nbsp;requesting one, along with the name of the event you are attending. This Lane Pass does not include on-site parking.</p>



<p class="kt-adv-heading1244_3165f7-7c_0 wp-block-kadence-advancedheading" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading1244_3165f7-7c_0"><strong>WeGo Access</strong></p>



<p class="kt-adv-heading1244_c37a6c-a9 wp-block-kadence-advancedheading" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading1244_c37a6c-a9">WeGo Access offers specialized vans for persons with disabilities within Davidson County who are unable to use fixed-route transit services. The cost is $3.70 per ride. Schedule 1 to 7 days in advance by calling 615-880-3970. Visit <a href="https://www.wegotransit.com/ride/transit-services/access/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">WeGoTransit.com<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a> to learn more.</p>



<p class="kt-adv-heading1244_3165f7-7c_2 wp-block-kadence-advancedheading" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading1244_3165f7-7c_2"></p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">For additional stadium accessibility information, please visit our&nbsp;<a href="https://nissanstadium.com/accessibility/">Accessibility</a> page.</p>

<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column1244_102cef-71"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col"></div></div>



<h2 class="kt-adv-heading1244_43abcf-eb wp-block-kadence-advancedheading" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading1244_43abcf-eb">CONCERTS &amp; NON-NFL EVENTS</h2>



<div class="wp-block-kadence-accordion alignnone"><div class="kt-accordion-wrap kt-accordion-id1244_51278e-4f kt-accordion-has-14-panes kt-active-pane-0 kt-accordion-block kt-pane-header-alignment-left kt-accodion-icon-style-arrow kt-accodion-icon-side-right" style="max-width:none"><div class="kt-accordion-inner-wrap" data-allow-multiple-open="false" data-start-open="none">
<div class="wp-block-kadence-pane kt-accordion-pane kt-accordion-pane-1 kt-pane1244_ae8ef5-7e"><div class="kt-accordion-header-wrap"><button class="kt-blocks-accordion-header kt-acccordion-button-label-show" type="button"><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-title-wrap"><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-title">ParkING</span></span><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-icon-trigger"></span></button></div><div class="kt-accordion-panel kt-accordion-panel-hidden" tabindex="0"><div class="kt-accordion-panel-inner">
<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column1244_22c156-5b"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<p class="wp-block-paragraph">On-site parking at Nissan Stadium is extremely limited and must be purchased in advance.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Explore nearby parking options:</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li>Find parking through our official partner, Parking Management Company (PMC),&nbsp;<a href="https://park.parkingmgt.com/v2/venues/nissan-stadium-nashville-tn" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">here<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a> until lots open for each event. After lots open, parking will be available to buy on-site at PMC lots until sold out.</li>



<li>Use the interactive campus map to view and book available PMC parking lots until they open for each event. <a href="https://qr.us.app.pam.co/s/5d493179" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Start here<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a>, find a lot, and click “Book Now.”</li>
</ul>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Guests with a Nissan Stadium parking passes can access turn-by-turn directions through <a href="https://qr.us.app.pam.co/s/5d493179" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">the interactive campus map here<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a>.</p>



<p class="kt-adv-heading1244_050fdf-01 wp-block-kadence-advancedheading" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading1244_050fdf-01"></p>



<p class="kt-adv-heading1244_f3c78f-7b wp-block-kadence-advancedheading" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading1244_f3c78f-7b"><em>Note: Each parking pass issued is valid for a single space in the designated lot.</em></p>
</div></div>
</div></div></div>



<div class="wp-block-kadence-pane kt-accordion-pane kt-accordion-pane-9 kt-pane1244_b3c080-cd"><div class="kt-accordion-header-wrap"><button class="kt-blocks-accordion-header kt-acccordion-button-label-show" type="button"><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-title-wrap"><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-title">WALK</span></span><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-icon-trigger"></span></button></div><div class="kt-accordion-panel kt-accordion-panel-hidden" tabindex="0"><div class="kt-accordion-panel-inner">
<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column1244_2c840f-d1"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Pedestrian access is available via multiple bridges connecting downtown and surrounding areas:</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><a href="https://qr.us.app.pam.co/s/1169572c" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Woodland Street Bridge<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a> <em>(recommended for fastest access)</em></li>



<li><a href="https://qr.us.app.pam.co/s/e241df24" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Korean Veterans Blvd Bridge<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a></li>



<li><a href="https://qr.us.app.pam.co/s/e6dcbb49" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">John Seigenthaler Pedestrian Bridge<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a></li>
</ul>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Walking is also available from East Nashville (Woodland St. &amp; Shelby Ave) and N. 1st Street.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph"><em>Note: Pedestrian routes may be impacted by construction. Follow posted signage.</em></p>
</div></div>
</div></div></div>



<div class="wp-block-kadence-pane kt-accordion-pane kt-accordion-pane-6 kt-pane1244_a8115c-10"><div class="kt-accordion-header-wrap"><button class="kt-blocks-accordion-header kt-acccordion-button-label-show" type="button"><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-title-wrap"><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-title">RIDESHARE</span></span><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-icon-trigger"></span></button></div><div class="kt-accordion-panel kt-accordion-panel-hidden" tabindex="0"><div class="kt-accordion-panel-inner">
<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column1244_183317-1f"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Several designated rideshare pick-up and drop-off locations are available within walking distance of the stadium. The two closest locations are <a href="https://short-url.us.app.pam.co/s/3cl4euvl" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Crutcher St on the South end<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a> and <a href="https://short-url.us.app.pam.co/s/hz64vfj0" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Gay St on the North end<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a> across Woodland St Bridge. More than a dozen additional designated zones are located on the downtown side of the river. Following each event, please follow posted signage or staff direction to access these locations safely.</p>



<p class="kt-adv-heading1244_5282a9-75 wp-block-kadence-advancedheading" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading1244_5282a9-75"><a href="https://nam12.safelinks.protection.outlook.com/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fshort-url.us.app.pam.co%2Fs%2Fcb9gc631&amp;data=05%7C02%7C%7Cf3bc18d0a821493a364408dec8339a99%7C7cd6ae7eca8c40838198ba5c0376357d%7C0%7C0%7C639168321146559554%7CUnknown%7CTWFpbGZsb3d8eyJFbXB0eU1hcGkiOnRydWUsIlYiOiIwLjAuMDAwMCIsIlAiOiJXaW4zMiIsIkFOIjoiTWFpbCIsIldUIjoyfQ%3D%3D%7C0%7C%7C%7C&amp;sdata=cAFO99PdrcP%2F%2FvOY4WtW9roYZd6hoTzPrey1NN6HcS0%3D&amp;reserved=0" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">See all designated Rideshare Zones here.<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a></p>



<p class="kt-adv-heading1244_80c999-0c wp-block-kadence-advancedheading" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading1244_80c999-0c"></p>
</div></div>
</div></div></div>



<div class="wp-block-kadence-pane kt-accordion-pane kt-accordion-pane-12 kt-pane1244_34c603-bd"><div class="kt-accordion-header-wrap"><button class="kt-blocks-accordion-header kt-acccordion-button-label-show" type="button"><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-title-wrap"><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-title">ELECTRIC SCOOTERS</span></span><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-icon-trigger"></span></button></div><div class="kt-accordion-panel kt-accordion-panel-hidden" tabindex="0"><div class="kt-accordion-panel-inner">
<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column1244_30b57e-f7"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<p class="kt-adv-heading1244_2cb2e8-f9 wp-block-kadence-advancedheading" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading1244_2cb2e8-f9">Scooter drop-off and pick-up locations are available at:<br></p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><a href="https://qr.us.app.pam.co/s/7bd95db6"></a><a href="https://qr.us.app.pam.co/s/9eb1ec42" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Corner of Shelby Ave &amp; S. 2nd St<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a></li>



<li><a href="https://qr.us.app.pam.co/s/e241df24" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener"><span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a><a href="https://qr.us.app.pam.co/s/894e00de"></a><a href="https://qr.us.app.pam.co/s/b64b6216" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Corner of Pedestrian Bridge &amp; S. 1st St.<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a></li>



<li><a href="https://qr.us.app.pam.co/s/f28176ce" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Corner of Woodland St &amp; S 1st St.<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a></li>



<li><a href="https://qr.us.app.pam.co/s/40cce369" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Woodland St near Lot M<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a></li>
</ul>



<p class="kt-adv-heading1244_8ad852-17 wp-block-kadence-advancedheading" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading1244_8ad852-17">Signage and scooter company representatives will direct fans where to leave and how to collect their scooter upon departure. Scooters are not permitted inside stadium perimeter areas.</p>
</div></div>
</div></div></div>



<div class="wp-block-kadence-pane kt-accordion-pane kt-accordion-pane-8 kt-pane1244_d4fcc6-45"><div class="kt-accordion-header-wrap"><button class="kt-blocks-accordion-header kt-acccordion-button-label-show" type="button"><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-title-wrap"><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-title">BIKE</span></span><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-icon-trigger"></span></button></div><div class="kt-accordion-panel kt-accordion-panel-hidden" tabindex="0"><div class="kt-accordion-panel-inner">
<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column1244_937146-cd"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<p class="kt-adv-heading1244_1dc5a7-7a wp-block-kadence-advancedheading" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading1244_1dc5a7-7a">Bike racks are available&nbsp;<a href="https://qr.us.app.pam.co/s/e59c8f91" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">across the street from Gate 6<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a>, <a href="https://qr.us.app.pam.co/s/cf3b1943" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">near Lot T<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a>, and <a href="https://short-url.us.app.pam.co/s/04w6ye61" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">on Woodland St near the pedestrian stairs<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a>. Nissan Stadium is not responsible for lost or stolen bikes.<br>&nbsp;<br><strong>Bike Valet by Walk Bike Nashville</strong><br>Nissan Stadium has partnered with&nbsp;<a href="https://www.walkbikenashville.org/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Walk Bike Nashville<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a>&nbsp;to provide free Bike Valet for all guests who ride their bikes to Nissan Stadium for select non-NFL events, including concerts. Check Walk Bike Nashville’s <a href="https://www.instagram.com/walkbikenash/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Instagram<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a> and <a href="https://www.facebook.com/walkbikenashville" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Facebook<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a> for details.</p>



<p class="kt-adv-heading1244_0cb2a3-fa wp-block-kadence-advancedheading" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading1244_0cb2a3-fa"><br>A blue Walk Bike Nashville tent will be set up at&nbsp;S. 1st St across from the foot of the pedestrian bridge&nbsp;.&nbsp;Bikes can be checked in up to one hour prior to the start of each event and checked out up to one hour after the end of each event. Guests using bike valet do not need to bring a bike lock.</p>
</div></div>
</div></div></div>



<div class="wp-block-kadence-pane kt-accordion-pane kt-accordion-pane-11 kt-pane1244_43a8d6-08"><div class="kt-accordion-header-wrap"><button class="kt-blocks-accordion-header kt-acccordion-button-label-show" type="button"><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-title-wrap"><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-title">Pedicabs</span></span><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-icon-trigger"></span></button></div><div class="kt-accordion-panel kt-accordion-panel-hidden" tabindex="0"><div class="kt-accordion-panel-inner">
<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column1244_959a28-44"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Pedicabs offer a convenient way to travel between the stadium and downtown, as well as between the stadium and designated rideshare pick-up and drop-off locations. After events, follow posted signage or staff direction to access pedicab zones located at:</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><a href="https://qr.us.app.pam.co/s/d2e60485" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Woodland St and S 1st St<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a></li>



<li><a href="https://qr.us.app.pam.co/s/eb9e1a67" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">The pedestrian bridge and S 1st St<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a></li>
</ul>
</div></div>
</div></div></div>



<div class="wp-block-kadence-pane kt-accordion-pane kt-accordion-pane-7 kt-pane1244_c964a0-a4"><div class="kt-accordion-header-wrap"><button class="kt-blocks-accordion-header kt-acccordion-button-label-show" type="button"><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-title-wrap"><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-title">GRAY LINE TENNESSEE EVENT SHUTTLE</span></span><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-icon-trigger"></span></button></div><div class="kt-accordion-panel kt-accordion-panel-hidden" tabindex="0"><div class="kt-accordion-panel-inner">
<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column1244_ea8386-37"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Enjoy a convenient and stress-free ride with the <a href="https://graylinetn.com/nissanstadium/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Gray Line Tennessee Nissan Stadium Event Shuttle<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a>.<br></p>



<ul class="wp-block-list has--font-size">
<li>Continuous service begins 90 minutes prior to event start time</li>



<li>Return shuttles begin at 10:00PM until 1 hour after event ends</li>



<li>Cost: $20 roundtrip per person (receipt serves as ticket)</li>



<li>Children 6 and under ride free</li>



<li>Cash is not accepted for this service, but QR codes will be available for event goers to purchase on-site next to parking lots</li>
</ul>



<p class="kt-adv-heading1244_d5f3b9-5f wp-block-kadence-advancedheading" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading1244_d5f3b9-5f"><em>Please note: Shuttle ticket purchase does not guarantee parking availability.</em><br></p>



<p class="kt-adv-heading1244_805031-c2 wp-block-kadence-advancedheading" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading1244_805031-c2"><a href="https://graylinetn.com/nissanstadium/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Purchase Gray Line Tennessee Nissan Stadium Event Shuttle Tickets here.<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a></p>
</div></div>
</div></div></div>



<div class="wp-block-kadence-pane kt-accordion-pane kt-accordion-pane-13 kt-pane1244_69bdd1-b7"><div class="kt-accordion-header-wrap"><button class="kt-blocks-accordion-header kt-acccordion-button-label-show" type="button"><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-title-wrap"><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-title">WEGO PUBLIC TRANSIT</span></span><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-icon-trigger"></span></button></div><div class="kt-accordion-panel kt-accordion-panel-hidden" tabindex="0"><div class="kt-accordion-panel-inner">
<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column1244_daff7f-b7"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Public transport through WeGo provides a convenient service to and from events hosted at Nissan Stadium. Options include Bus Service with four direct service routes to a conveniently-located stop on <a href="https://qr.us.app.pam.co/s/333a84d9" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Woodland St at the corner of S. 1st St<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a> as well as park and ride from the Elizabeth Duff Transit Center.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Visit <a href="https://www.wegotransit.com/community/nissan-stadium/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">WeGoTransit.com/Community/Nissan-Stadium<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a> for all the details!</p>
</div></div>
</div></div></div>



<div class="wp-block-kadence-pane kt-accordion-pane kt-accordion-pane-10 kt-pane1244_c73d8d-41"><div class="kt-accordion-header-wrap"><button class="kt-blocks-accordion-header kt-acccordion-button-label-show" type="button"><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-title-wrap"><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-title">ACCESSIBILE OPTIONS</span></span><span class="kt-blocks-accordion-icon-trigger"></span></button></div><div class="kt-accordion-panel kt-accordion-panel-hidden" tabindex="0"><div class="kt-accordion-panel-inner">
<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column1244_700abf-37"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<p class="wp-block-paragraph"><strong>Accessible Parking</strong></p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Accessible parking is available in stadium lots with a valid parking pass and is offered on a first-come, first-served basis. Parking passes must be&nbsp;<a href="https://seatgeek.com/venues/nissan-stadium-1-titans-way/parking" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">purchased in advance<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a>.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph"><strong>Accessible Drop-Off &amp; Pick-Up in Lot T</strong></p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">ADA drop-off and pick-up is <a href="https://qr.us.app.pam.co/s/e101ebfc" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">located in Lot T<span class="screen-reader-text">opens in a new tab</span></a>. Directions:</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li>Travel southbound on S. 5th St from Shelby Ave</li>



<li>Turn right onto Davidson St and proceed to Lot T</li>
</ul>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Please have your stadium-issued ADA Lane Pass ready for law enforcement at roadblocks. To receive a stadium-issued ADA Lane pass, please&nbsp;<a href="mailto:stadiumexperienceinfo@titans.nfl.com">send us a message</a>&nbsp;requesting one, along with the name of the event you are attending. This Lane Pass does not include on-site parking.</p>



<p class="kt-adv-heading1244_15f2e5-a9_0 wp-block-kadence-advancedheading" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading1244_15f2e5-a9_0"><strong>WeGo Access</strong></p>



<p class="kt-adv-heading1244_15f2e5-a9_1 wp-block-kadence-advancedheading" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading1244_15f2e5-a9_1">WeGo Access offers specialized vans for persons with disabilities within Davidson County who are unable to use fixed-route transit services. The cost is $3.70 per ride. Schedule 1 to 7 days in advance by calling 615-880-3970. Visit WeGoTransit.com to learn more.</p>



<p class="kt-adv-heading1244_9da31f-4d wp-block-kadence-advancedheading" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading1244_9da31f-4d"></p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">For additional stadium accessibility information, please visit our&nbsp;<a href="https://nissanstadium.com/accessibility/">Accessibility</a> page.</p>
</div></div>
</div></div></div>
</div></div></div>
</div></div>

</div></div>

<div class="wp-block-kadence-column kadence-column1244_257f60-0b"><div class="kt-inside-inner-col">
<h2 class="kt-adv-heading1244_b62ed9-d3 wp-block-kadence-advancedheading" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading1244_b62ed9-d3">Campus Map</h2>



<p class="kt-adv-heading1244_80f6e1-00 wp-block-kadence-advancedheading" data-kb-block="kb-adv-heading1244_80f6e1-00">Use this 3D interactive, smart navigation map of the Nissan Stadium campus. Get real-time directions from your home, hotel, or anywhere to your seating section, plus easily find concessions, restrooms, and other amenities closest to your seat. Access online anytime on the go.</p>

<script type="text/javascript">
    
     // lets the user close the nav dropdown pressing Escape key
	const primaryMenuItems = document.querySelectorAll('#primary-menu .menu-item-has-children');
    primaryMenuItems.forEach(menuItem => {      
        menuItem.addEventListener('mouseenter', (e) => {
            menuItem.querySelector('.sub-menu')?.classList.add('submenu-open');
        });
        menuItem.addEventListener('mouseleave', (e) => {
            menuItem.querySelector('.sub-menu')?.classList.remove('submenu-open');
        });
    });
    document.onkeydown = function(e) {
        e = e || window.event;
        const menuItemActive = document.querySelector('#primary-menu .menu-item-has-children .sub-menu.submenu-open');
        
        if (e.keyCode == 27 && menuItemActive) {                    
            menuItemActive.classList.contains('submenu-open') ? menuItemActive.classList.remove('submenu-open') : null;         
        }
    };
	
	// adds aria-hidden true to svg icons
    window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {		
        const svgIconsets = document.querySelectorAll('.kadence-svg-iconset, .kb-svg-icon-wrap');
        svgIconsets.forEach(svgIconset => {
        	svgIconset.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
        });
    })
    
    // Moves the focus to the first input with error when click on submit
    const formItems = document.querySelectorAll('.gform_wrapper');
    formItems.forEach(formItem => {
    	const formItemSubmit = formItem.querySelector('input[type=submit]');
    	formItemSubmit.addEventListener('click', (e) => {    		
        	sessionStorage.setItem("reloading", "true");        	
    	})    	
	    window.onload = function() {
	    	let reloading = sessionStorage.getItem("reloading");
	        if (reloading) {
	            sessionStorage.removeItem("reloading");
	            const formItemError = formItem.querySelector('.gfield_error input');
	            formItemError?.focus();
	        }	    	
	    }	   
    })
    
    // indicates the user that this link onpens in a new tab
    //window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {		
	//	const outerLinks = document.querySelectorAll('[target="_blank"]:not(.kb-section-link-overlay)');	
	//	outerLinks.forEach(outerLink => {
	//		let linkText = outerLink.textContent;
	//		outerLink.setAttribute('aria-label', linkText + ' - opens in a new tab');

	 window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {		
		const outerLinks = document.querySelectorAll('[target="_blank"]');	
		outerLinks.forEach(outerLink => {
			let textElement = document.createElement("span");
			textElement.innerHTML ="opens in a new tab";
			textElement.classList.add("screen-reader-text");
			outerLink.append(textElement);		
		})
	})
	
	window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {		
		const outerLinks = document.querySelectorAll('.cky-footer-wrapper [target="_blank"]');	
		outerLinks.forEach(outerLink => {
			let linkText = outerLink.textContent;
			outerLink.setAttribute('aria-label', 'cookie yes - opens in a new tab');		
		})
	})
	
	window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {		
		const cartNumber = document.querySelectorAll('.header-cart-button');	
		cartNumber.forEach(cartNumber => {
			let linkText = cartNumber.textContent;
			cartNumber.setAttribute('aria-label', linkText + ' items');		
		})
	})
	
	
	// removing role="note" from slider 
	const n2Slides = document.querySelectorAll('.n2-section-smartslider [role="note"]');
	for (let i = 0; i < n2Slides.length; i++) {
	    n2Slides[i].removeAttribute('role');
    }
    
    // Allows for floating label in gravity forms
    const formUi = {
        init: function() {
          const formElements = document.querySelectorAll(".floating-label input, .floating-label textarea, .floating-label select");
      
          formElements.forEach(function(element) {
            element.addEventListener("blur", function() {
              const input = this;
              const parent = input.parentNode;
      
              if (input.value !== "" && (input.tagName !== "SELECT" || input.options[input.selectedIndex].text !== "- Select -")) {
                parent.classList.add("input-active");
              } else {
                parent.classList.remove("input-active");
              }
            });
          });
        }
      };
      
    
    
    // trap focus inside modal
    const focusableElements = 'button, [href], input';
    const modals = document.querySelectorAll('.cky-modal, #mobile-drawer, #search-drawer, .kb-search-modal, .kb-search-modal'); 
    modals.forEach(modal => {
        const focusableContent = modal.querySelectorAll(focusableElements);
    
        if (focusableContent.length === 0) return;
        
        const firstFocusableElement = focusableContent[0]; // get first element to be focused inside modal
        const lastFocusableElement = focusableContent[focusableContent.length - 1]; // get last element to be focused inside modal
        
        
        document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
          let isTabPressed = e.key === 'Tab' || e.keyCode === 9;
          if (!isTabPressed) {
            return;
          }
        
          if (e.shiftKey) { // if shift key pressed for shift + tab combination
            if (document.activeElement === firstFocusableElement) {
              lastFocusableElement.focus(); // add focus for the last focusable element
              e.preventDefault();
            }
          } else { // if tab key is pressed
            if (document.activeElement === lastFocusableElement) { // if focused has reached to last focusable element then focus first focusable element after pressing tab
              firstFocusableElement.focus(); // add focus for the first focusable element
              e.preventDefault();
            }
          }
      });
        
        firstFocusableElement.focus();
    });
      
    


// close video when modal is closed
class Modal {
        constructor(el) {
            if (!el) return;
            
            this.el = el;
            this.closeBtn = this.el.querySelector('.kt-modal-close');
            this.overlay = this.el.querySelector('.kt-modal-overlay');
            this.modalContent = this.el.querySelector('.kt-modal-content');
            this.videoWrapper = this.el.querySelector('.wp-block-embed-youtube');
            this.videoWrapperClone = this.videoWrapper?.cloneNode(true);

            this.setupEventHandlers();
        }
        
        setupEventHandlers() {
            this.onClose();
        }

        onClose() {
            this.closeBtn?.addEventListener('click', this.stopVideo.bind(this));
            this.closeBtn?.addEventListener('touchend', this.stopVideo.bind(this));

            this.overlay?.addEventListener('click', this.onOverlayClick.bind(this));
            this.overlay?.addEventListener('touchend', this.onOverlayClick.bind(this));
        }
        
        onOverlayClick(event) {
            if (event.target === this.overlay) {
                this.stopVideo();
            }
        }
        
        stopVideo() {
            if (!this.videoWrapper) return;
    
            this.modalContent.removeChild(this.videoWrapper);
            this.videoWrapper = this.videoWrapperClone;

            this.modalContent.appendChild(this.videoWrapper);
        }
        
    }
    
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {        
        document.querySelectorAll('.wp-block-kadence-modal').forEach(el => new Modal(el));
    });

        function onCloseModalRestoreFocus(eventTarget) {
            const modalId = $(eventTarget).closest('.kadence-block-pro-modal').attr('id');

            if (modalId) {
                const $openButton = $(`[data-modal-open="${modalId}"]`);
                setTimeout(() => $openButton.focus(), 200);
            }
        }

            e = e || window.event;

            if (e.keyCode == 27) {  
                onCloseModalRestoreFocus(e.target);
            }
        });
